  5.  MODIFICATIONS

            
    
修改
    You may copy and distribute a Modified Version of the Document under
                the conditions of sections 2 and 3 above, provided that you release
                the Modified Version under precisely this License, with the Modified
                Version filling the role of the Document, thus licensing distribution
                and modification of the Modified Version to whoever possesses a copy
                of it. In addition, you must do these things in the Modified Version:
            
    

    您可以按上述第 2、3 节的条件复制和分发文档的修改版本，但必须严格按本许可证发布修改版本，并以修改版本作为本许可证所指的“文档”，从而允许任何持有副本的人分发和修改该修改版本。此外，还必须在修改版本中完成以下事项：
    
            
      
                
    1.  Use in the Title Page (and on the covers, if any) a title distinct
                    from that of the Document, and from those of previous versions
                    (which should, if there were any, be listed in the History section
                    of the Document). You may use the same title as a previous version
                    if the original publisher of that version gives permission.

                
      
在扉页（以及封面，如果有）使用与本文档及其先前版本不同的标题；先前版本如存在，应在文档的历史章节中列出。经某一先前版本的原出版者许可，也可以使用与该版本相同的标题。
      2. 
                
    2.  List on the Title Page, as authors, one or more persons or entities
                    responsible for authorship of the modifications in the Modified
                    Version, together with at least five of the principal authors of the
                    Document (all of its principal authors, if it has fewer than five),
                    unless they release you from this requirement.

                
      
在扉页上列出一位或多位负责修改版本内容的个人或实体，作为作者；同时列出本文档至少五位主要作者，不足五位时列出全部，除非他们免除这一要求。
      3. 
                
    3.  State on the Title page the name of the publisher of the
                    Modified Version, as the publisher.

                
      
在扉页上以出版者身份注明修改版本出版者的名称。
      4. 
                
    4.  Preserve all the copyright notices of the Document.

                
      
保留本文档的所有版权声明。
      5. 
                
    5.  Add an appropriate copyright notice for your modifications
                    adjacent to the other copyright notices.

                
      
在其他版权声明旁，为自己的修改增加适当的版权声明。
      6. 
                
    6.  Include, immediately after the copyright notices, a license notice
                    giving the public permission to use the Modified Version under the
                    terms of this License, in the form shown in the Addendum below.

                
      
在版权声明之后立即加入许可声明，允许公众按本许可证的条款使用修改版本；格式见后面的附录。
      7. 
                
    7.  Preserve in that license notice the full lists of Invariant Sections
                    and required Cover Texts given in the Document’s license notice.

                
      
在许可声明中完整保留原文档许可声明所列的不变章节和必需封面文字清单。
      8. 
                
    8.  Include an unaltered copy of this License.

                
      
附上本许可证未经改动的完整副本。
      9. 
                
    9.  Preserve the section Entitled “History”, Preserve its Title, and add
                    to it an item stating at least the title, year, new authors, and
                    publisher of the Modified Version as given on the Title Page. If
                    there is no section Entitled “History” in the Document, create one
                    stating the title, year, authors, and publisher of the Document as
                    given on its Title Page, then add an item describing the Modified
                    Version as stated in the previous sentence.

                
      
保留标题为“历史（History）”的章节及其标题，并增加一项记录，至少写明扉页上给出的修改版本标题、年份、新作者和出版者。若原文档没有该章节，则创建一章，先按原扉页记录原文档的标题、年份、作者和出版者，再按前句要求加入修改版本记录。
      10. 
                
    10.  Preserve the network location, if any, given in the Document for
                    public access to a Transparent copy of the Document, and likewise
                    the network locations given in the Document for previous versions
                    it was based on. These may be placed in the “History” section.
                    You may omit a network location for a work that was published at
                    least four years before the Document itself, or if the original
                    publisher of the version it refers to gives permission.

                
      
保留文档所列的、供公众获取透明副本的网络位置（如果有），以及其所依据的先前版本的网络位置；可以将它们放在历史章节中。如果某作品比本文档至少早四年出版，或者相关版本的原出版者允许，可以省略其网络位置。
      11. 
                
    11.  For any section Entitled “Acknowledgements” or “Dedications”, Preserve
                    the Title of the section, and preserve in the section all the
                    substance and tone of each of the contributor acknowledgements and/or
                    dedications given therein.

                
      
保留所有标题为“致谢（Acknowledgements）”或“献辞（Dedications）”的章节标题，并保留其中各项贡献者致谢和／或献辞的全部实质内容及语气。
      12. 
                
    12.  Preserve all the Invariant Sections of the Document,
                    unaltered in their text and in their titles. Section numbers
                    or the equivalent are not considered part of the section titles.

                
      
原样保留本文档的所有不变章节，其正文和标题均不得改动。章节编号或同等标记不视为标题的一部分。
      13. 
                
    13.  Delete any section Entitled “Endorsements”. Such a section
                    may not be included in the Modified Version.

                
      
删除所有标题为“认可（Endorsements）”的原有章节。修改版本中不得包含这样的章节。
      14. 
                
    14.  Do not retitle any existing section to be Entitled “Endorsements” or
                    to conflict in title with any Invariant Section.

                
      
不得将任何现有章节改名为“认可（Endorsements）”，也不得改成与任何不变章节冲突的标题。
      15. 
                
    15.  Preserve any Warranty Disclaimers.
                
      
保留所有担保免责声明。
      16. 
            
    

            
    If the Modified Version includes new front-matter sections or
                appendices that qualify as Secondary Sections and contain no material
                copied from the Document, you may at your option designate some or all
                of these sections as invariant. To do this, add their titles to the
                list of Invariant Sections in the Modified Version’s license notice.
                These titles must be distinct from any other section titles.
            
    

    如果修改版本新增的前置章节或附录符合“次要章节”的定义，且这些新增章节不含从原文档复制的材料，您可以自行选择将其中部分或全部指定为不变章节。为此，应将它们的标题添加到修改版本许可声明的不变章节清单中。这些标题必须与其他所有章节标题不同。
    
            
    You may add a section Entitled “Endorsements”, provided it contains
                nothing but endorsements of your Modified Version by various
                parties—for example, statements of peer review or that the text has
                been approved by an organization as the authoritative definition of a
                standard.
            
    

    您可以新增标题为“认可（Endorsements）”的章节，但其中只能包含各方对您的修改版本的认可，例如同行评审意见，或某组织已认可该文本作为某项标准权威定义的声明。
    
            
    You may add a passage of up to five words as a Front-Cover Text, and a
                passage of up to 25 words as a Back-Cover Text, to the end of the list
                of Cover Texts in the Modified Version. Only one passage of
                Front-Cover Text and one of Back-Cover Text may be added by (or
                through arrangements made by) any one entity. If the Document already
                includes a cover text for the same cover, previously added by you or
                by arrangement made by the same entity you are acting on behalf of,
                you may not add another; but you may replace the old one, on explicit
                permission from the previous publisher that added the old one.
            
    

    您可以在修改版本的封面文字清单末尾，增加一段最多 5 个单词的正封面文字，以及一段最多 25 个单词的封底文字。每个实体自行或通过其安排，至多可以各增加一段正封面文字和封底文字。如果文档中已为同一封面包含了由您，或由您所代表的同一实体安排添加的文字，就不得再增加一段；但经添加旧文字的前一出版者明确许可，可以替换旧文字。
    
            
    The author(s) and publisher(s) of the Document do not by this License
                give permission to use their names for publicity for or to assert or
                imply endorsement of any Modified Version.
            
    

    本许可证并未授权您使用文档作者或出版者的姓名或名称来宣传修改版本，或声称、暗示他们认可任何修改版本。
    
        
    6. 
        
  6.  COMBINING DOCUMENTS

            
    
合并文档
    You may combine the Document with other documents released under this
                License, under the terms defined in section 4 above for modified
                versions, provided that you include in the combination all of the
                Invariant Sections of all of the original documents, unmodified, and
                list them all as Invariant Sections of your combined work in its
                license notice, and that you preserve all their Warranty Disclaimers.
            
    

    您可以按上述第 4 节关于修改版本的条款，将本文档与按本许可证发布的其他文档合并，但必须在合并作品中原样纳入所有原文档的不变章节，并在合并作品的许可声明中全部列为不变章节，同时保留所有担保免责声明。
    
            
    The combined work need only contain one copy of this License, and
                multiple identical Invariant Sections may be replaced with a single
                copy. If there are multiple Invariant Sections with the same name but
                different contents, make the title of each such section unique by
                adding at the end of it, in parentheses, the name of the original
                author or publisher of that section if known, or else a unique number.
                Make the same adjustment to the section titles in the list of
                Invariant Sections in the license notice of the combined work.
            
    

    合并作品只需包含本许可证的一份副本，多个完全相同的不变章节可以只保留一份。如果有多个名称相同、内容却不同的不变章节，应在每个标题末尾加上括号：已知该章节原作者或出版者时，在括号中填写其姓名或名称；否则填写唯一编号，从而使各标题互不相同。合并作品许可声明中的不变章节清单，也必须对相应标题作出同样调整。
    
            
    In the combination, you must combine any sections Entitled “History”
                in the various original documents, forming one section Entitled
                “History”; likewise combine any sections Entitled “Acknowledgements”,
                and any sections Entitled “Dedications”. You must delete all
                sections Entitled “Endorsements.”
            
    

    合并时，必须将各原文档中标题为“历史（History）”的章节合并为一个同名章节；同样合并标题为“致谢（Acknowledgements）”的章节及标题为“献辞（Dedications）”的章节。必须删除所有标题为“认可（Endorsements）”的章节。
    
        
    7. 
        
  7.  COLLECTIONS OF DOCUMENTS

            
    
文档合集
    You may make a collection consisting of the Document and other documents
                released under this License, and replace the individual copies of this
                License in the various documents with a single copy that is included in
                the collection, provided that you follow the rules of this License for
                verbatim copying of each of the documents in all other respects.
            
    

    您可以将本文档与其他按本许可证发布的文档组成合集，并用合集内的一份许可证副本，替代各文档中单独附带的许可证副本；前提是在其他所有方面，都遵守本许可证关于逐字复制各文档的规则。
    
            
    You may extract a single document from such a collection, and distribute
                it individually under this License, provided you insert a copy of this
                License into the extracted document, and follow this License in all
                other respects regarding verbatim copying of that document.
            
    

    您可以从这样的合集中提取单个文档，并按本许可证单独分发；前提是给提取出的文档附上许可证副本，并在逐字复制该文档的其他所有方面遵守本许可证。
    
        
    8. 
        
  8.  AGGREGATION WITH INDEPENDENT WORKS

            
    
与独立作品的汇编
    A compilation of the Document or its derivatives with other separate
                and independent documents or works, in or on a volume of a storage or
                distribution medium, is called an “aggregate” if the copyright
                resulting from the compilation is not used to limit the legal rights
                of the compilation’s users beyond what the individual works permit.
                When the Document is included in an aggregate, this License does not
                apply to the other works in the aggregate which are not themselves
                derivative works of the Document.
            
    

    在一个存储或分发媒介单元中，将本文档或其衍生作品与其他彼此独立的文档或作品汇编在一起，如果不利用汇编产生的版权，将用户的合法权利限制到各单独作品许可范围之外，则称为“汇编体”。本文档被收入汇编体时，本许可证不适用于其中本身并非本文档衍生作品的其他作品。
    
            
    If the Cover Text requirement of section 3 is applicable to these
                copies of the Document, then if the Document is less than one half of
                the entire aggregate, the Document’s Cover Texts may be placed on
                covers that bracket the Document within the aggregate, or the
                electronic equivalent of covers if the Document is in electronic form.
                Otherwise they must appear on printed covers that bracket the whole
                aggregate.
            
    

    如果第 3 节的封面文字要求适用于这些文档副本，那么当文档不足整个汇编体的一半时，可以将指定封面文字放在汇编体内部包围本文档的封面上；若为电子文档，则可放在对应的电子封面上。否则，这些文字必须出现在包围整个汇编体的印刷封面上。
    
        
    9. 
        
  9.  TRANSLATION

            
    
翻译
    Translation is considered a kind of modification, so you may
                distribute translations of the Document under the terms of section 4.
                Replacing Invariant Sections with translations requires special
                permission from their copyright holders, but you may include
                translations of some or all Invariant Sections in addition to the
                original versions of these Invariant Sections. You may include a
                translation of this License, and all the license notices in the
                Document, and any Warranty Disclaimers, provided that you also include
                the original English version of this License and the original versions
                of those notices and disclaimers. In case of a disagreement between
                the translation and the original version of this License or a notice
                or disclaimer, the original version will prevail.
            
    

    翻译被视为一种修改，因此可以按第 4 节的条款分发文档译本。用译文替换不变章节，需要得到相关版权人的特别许可；不过，可以保留这些章节的原版，并附上其中部分或全部章节的译文。您可以附上本许可证、文档中所有许可声明及任何担保免责声明的译文，前提是同时保留本许可证的英文原版，以及这些声明和免责声明的原版。若许可证、声明或免责声明的译文与原版不一致，以原版为准。
    
            
    If a section in the Document is Entitled “Acknowledgements”,
                “Dedications”, or “History”, the requirement (section 4) to Preserve
                its Title (section 1) will typically require changing the actual
                title.
            
    

    如果文档中的章节标题为“致谢（Acknowledgements）”“献辞（Dedications）”或“历史（History）”，那么第 4 节要求按第 1 节的定义“保留标题”，通常意味着需要调整实际标题。

  10.  TERMINATION

            
    
终止
    You may not copy, modify, sublicense, or distribute the Document
                except as expressly provided under this License. Any attempt
                otherwise to copy, modify, sublicense, or distribute it is void, and
                will automatically terminate your rights under this License.
            
    

    除本许可证明确允许的情形外，不得复制、修改、再许可或分发本文档。任何其他方式的复制、修改、再许可或分发尝试均为无效，并会自动终止您在本许可证下的权利。
    
            
    However, if you cease all violation of this License, then your license
                from a particular copyright holder is reinstated (a) provisionally,
                unless and until the copyright holder explicitly and finally
                terminates your license, and (b) permanently, if the copyright holder
                fails to notify you of the violation by some reasonable means prior to
                60 days after the cessation.
            
    

    不过，如果您停止一切违反本许可证的行为，则来自某一特定版权人的许可将得到恢复：(a) 暂时恢复，除非该版权人明确且最终地终止您的许可；若其作出这样的终止，暂时恢复即结束；(b) 如果该版权人在您停止违规后的 60 天期满之前，未通过某种合理方式通知您违规事项，则永久恢复。
    
            
    Moreover, your license from a particular copyright holder is
                reinstated permanently if the copyright holder notifies you of the
                violation by some reasonable means, this is the first time you have
                received notice of violation of this License (for any work) from that
                copyright holder, and you cure the violation prior to 30 days after
                your receipt of the notice.
            
    

    此外，如果某一特定版权人以某种合理方式通知您违规事项，而这是您首次收到该版权人针对您违反本许可证行为所发的通知（无论涉及哪部作品），并且您在收到通知后 30 天内纠正违规行为，那么来自该版权人的许可将永久恢复。
    
            
    Termination of your rights under this section does not terminate the
                licenses of parties who have received copies or rights from you under
                this License. If your rights have been terminated and not permanently
                reinstated, receipt of a copy of some or all of the same material does
                not give you any rights to use it.
            
    

    终止您在本节下的权利不会终止已收到您根据本许可提供的副本或权利的各方的许可。如果您的权利已终止且未永久恢复，则收到部分或全部相同材料的副本并不赋予您使用该材料的任何权利。

本许可证的未来修订
    The Free Software Foundation may publish new, revised versions

    自由软件基金会可能不时发布 GNU 自由文档许可证的新修订版本。新版本的精神与本版本相似，但可能为应对新问题或关切而在细节上有所不同。参见 GNU copyleft。
    
            
    Each version of the License is given a distinguishing version number.
                If the Document specifies that a particular numbered version of this
                License “or any later version” applies to it, you have the option of
                following the terms and conditions either of that specified version or
                of any later version that has been published (not as a draft) by the
                Free Software Foundation. If the Document does not specify a version

                specifies that a proxy can decide which future versions of this
                License can be used, that proxy’s public statement of acceptance of a
                version permanently authorizes you to choose that version for the

    每个版本的许可证都有独立的版本号。如果文档指定本许可证的某个版本“或任何后续版本”适用，您可以选择遵守该版本，或自由软件基金会已正式发布而非草案的任何后续版本的条款。如果文档没有指定版本号，您可以选择自由软件基金会曾正式发布的任何版本。如果文档指定由某个代理人决定可使用哪些未来版本，那么该代理人公开声明接受某个版本，就永久授权您为该文档选择该版本。

  12.  RELICENSING

            
    
重新许可
    “Massive Multiauthor Collaboration Site” (or “MMC Site”) means any
                World Wide Web server that publishes copyrightable works and also
                provides prominent facilities for anybody to edit those works. A
                public wiki that anybody can edit is an example of such a server. A
                “Massive Multiauthor Collaboration” (or “MMC”) contained in the
                site means any set of copyrightable works thus published on the MMC
                site.
            
    

    “大型多作者协作网站”（MMC Site）是指发布可受版权保护的作品，并提供醒目的设施供任何人编辑这些作品的万维网服务器。任何人均可编辑的公共维基就是一个例子。网站中的“大型多作者协作作品集合”（MMC），是指以这种方式在该网站上发布的任意一组可受版权保护的作品。
    
            
    “CC-BY-SA” means the Creative Commons Attribution-Share Alike 3.0
                license published by Creative Commons Corporation, a not-for-profit
                corporation with a principal place of business in San Francisco,
                California, as well as future copyleft versions of that license
                published by that same organization.
            
    

    “CC-BY-SA”是指由 Creative Commons Corporation（一家主要营业地点位于加利福尼亚州旧金山的非营利性公司）发布的知识共享署名-相同方式共享 3.0 许可证，以及该组织发布的该许可证的未来 Copyleft 版本。
    
            
    “Incorporate” means to publish or republish a Document, in whole or
                in part, as part of another Document.
            
    

    “纳入”是指将某文档的全部或部分作为另一文档的一部分，予以发布或重新发布。
    
            
    An MMC is “eligible for relicensing” if it is licensed under this
                License, and if all works that were first published under this License
                somewhere other than this MMC, and subsequently incorporated in whole
                or in part into the MMC, (1) had no cover texts or invariant sections,
                and (2) were thus incorporated prior to November 1, 2008.
            
    

    某个 MMC 若按本许可证获得许可，并且所有先在该 MMC 以外按本许可证发布、后来全部或部分纳入该 MMC 的作品，同时满足 (1) 没有封面文字，也没有不变章节；(2) 在 2008 年 11 月 1 日之前即已纳入，则该 MMC“符合重新许可资格”。
    
            
    The operator of an MMC Site may republish an MMC contained in the site
                under CC-BY-SA on the same site at any time before August 1, 2009,
                provided the MMC is eligible for relicensing.
            
    

    MMC 网站的运营者可以在 2009 年 8 月 1 日之前的任何时间，在同一网站上按 CC-BY-SA 重新发布网站中的某个 MMC，前提是该 MMC 符合重新许可的条件。

附录：如何将本许可证用于您的文档

如果存在不变章节、正封面文字和封底文字，请将上述从“with”开始、到“Texts.”结束的文字替换为：

    

        
    with the Invariant Sections being list their titles, with

    being list.

封底文字为：列出文字。

        combination of the three, merge those two alternatives to suit the
        situation.

Footnotes


脚注


        
(1)

        
C++ also has complex number support, but it is
            incompatible with the ISO C99 types.


C++ 也支持复数，但与 ISO C99 的复数类型不兼容。

        
(2)

        
a full declarator is a declaration of a function or an object which is not part of another object


完整声明符是指不属于另一对象一部分的函数或对象的声明。

        
(3)

        
However if for example MAX is
            INT_MAX and x is of type int, we clearly have a
            problem with overflow. See Overflow.
        


不过，例如当 MAX 为 INT_MAX 且 x 为 int 类型时，就显然存在溢出问题。参见溢出。

        
(4)

        
Rarely, argv[0] can be a
            null pointer (in
            this case argc is 0) or argv[0][0] can be the null character.
            In any case, argv[argc] is a null pointer.


极少数情况下，argv[0] 可以是空指针（此时 argc 为 0），或者 argv[0][0] 可以是空字符。无论哪种情况，argv[argc] 都是空指针。
